   111	
   112	## 3. `assets/api.js` — fetch wrapper, WS client, invalidation bus
   113	
   114	Attaches `Planner.api` and `Planner.bus`; defines `window.__plannerDebug`. IIFE, `"use strict"`.
   115	
   116	### `Planner.api.fetchJson(path, options) -> Promise<object>`
   117	
   118	- `options` optional: `{ method?: string, body?: object }`. When `body` present: `JSON.stringify` it, header `Content-Type: application/json`. Default method GET.
   119	- 2xx → `response.json()`; a JSON parse failure rejects with `code:"bad_json"`.
   120	- Non-2xx → read body, try `JSON.parse`; if it carries the PlannerError envelope (`parsed.error && parsed.error.code`) reject with an `Error` whose `message = parsed.error.message` and with properties `code = parsed.error.code`, `detail = parsed.error.detail`, `status = response.status`, `isPlannerError = true` (errors.py `to_payload()` shape: `{"error":{code,message,detail}}`). Otherwise reject with `code:"http_error"`, `message:"HTTP " + status`, `status`.
   121	- Network-level failure (fetch rejects) → normalized to `code:"network"`, `message:"network error"`. Every rejection therefore carries `code` + `message`, which is exactly what `errorLine` renders (D11 item 17).
   122	
   123	### `Planner.bus` — the invalidation bus (SPEC §9 audit target)
   124	
   125	State (the complete list — nothing else): `cursor` (int, starts 0), `socket`, `debounceMs` (set by `start`), `flushTimer`, `retryMs` (starts `WS_RETRY_MIN_MS`), `onFlush` (single callback), `started` (bool guard).
   126	
   127	- `Planner.bus.onInvalidate(fn)` — registers THE single flush callback (last write wins; `app.js` is the only caller).
   128	- `Planner.bus.start(debounceMs)` — stores `debounceMs`, sets `started`, calls internal `connect()`. Idempotent (second call ignored).
   129	- `connect()`: `new WebSocket((location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss://" : "ws://") + location.host + Planner.config.API.EVENTS_WS + "?since=" + cursor)`.
   130	  - `onopen`: `retryMs = WS_RETRY_MIN_MS`; `__plannerDebug.wsOpens += 1`; one quiet `console.debug("[planner] ws open since=" + cursor)`.
   131	  - `onmessage`: `var msg = JSON.parse(e.data)` inside try/catch (parse failure → one `console.debug`, return — the bus never throws). Then **the only field ever read is `msg.cursor`**: `if (typeof msg.cursor === "number") { cursor = msg.cursor; __plannerDebug.cursor = cursor; }` and `schedule()`. `msg.events` is never inspected, stored, or reconciled — the batch is purely an invalidation signal.
   132	  - `schedule()`: `clearTimeout(flushTimer); flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, debounceMs)` — classic trailing debounce. (Server-side poll is `ws_poll_ms` (default 300ms) per ws.py, so distinct batches arrive ≥ poll apart; back-to-back full-batch continuations coalesce into one flush, which is the point. Starvation is impossible: quiet gap ≥ poll interval > nothing; the timer always fires.)
   133	  - `flush()`: `flushTimer = null; __plannerDebug.flushes += 1; console.debug("[planner] flush " + __plannerDebug.flushes); if (onFlush) onFlush();` — ONE quiet line per flush, the console hook the ticket sanctions.
   134	  - `onclose` (reconnect handled here only — `onerror` is always followed by `close`, handling both would double-schedule): `socket = null; setTimeout(connect, retryMs); retryMs = Math.min(retryMs * WS_RETRY_FACTOR, WS_RETRY_MAX_MS)`. Reconnect reuses the current `cursor` in `?since=` — ws.py replays every event row with id > cursor, so nothing is missed and nothing needs client buffering.
   135	
   136	### Debug hook (sanctioned by ticket acceptance "observable via console hook")
   137	
   138	```js
   139	window.__plannerDebug = { flushes: 0, wsOpens: 0, cursor: 0 };
   140	```
   141	Counters only; one `console.debug` line per flush and per socket open; nothing else logs.
   142	
   143	### Event → debounce → refetch sequence (normative)
   144	
   145	```
   146	writer appends events row(s)                                   (any mutating endpoint)
   147	  └─ ≤ ws_poll_ms later: ws.py poll finds rows > cursor
   148	       └─ push {events:[...], cursor: N}
   149	client onmessage
   150	  ├─ cursor := N                     ← the ONLY thing read from the batch
   151	  └─ schedule(): clearTimeout; setTimeout(flush, debounceMs)
   152	       [more batches inside the window merely reset the timer → they coalesce]
   153	timer fires → flush()
   154	  ├─ __plannerDebug.flushes += 1 ; console.debug("[planner] flush n")
   155	  └─ onFlush()                       ← exactly one notification, app.js's handler:
   156	        ├─ refreshBadge(): GET /api/queues → shell.setReviewBadge(approvals.length)
   157	        └─ route(): re-render active screen — render() refetches full JSON, no cache
   158	socket drops
   159	  └─ onclose: setTimeout(connect, retryMs); retryMs := min(retryMs×2, 10000)
   160	connect() → GET ws /api/events?since=<cursor>   (resume; server replays > cursor)
   161	onopen → retryMs := 500 ; wsOpens += 1
   162	```
   163	`debounceMs` = `meta.ui_debounce_ms` (250 from config.py); until `/api/meta` answers, `DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT` (250) from config.js. Backoff parameters all from `Planner.config`.
   164	
   165	## 4. `assets/markdown.js` — minimal safe renderer
   166	
   167	Attaches `Planner.markdown`. One public function:
   168	
   169	`Planner.markdown.render(text) -> HTMLElement` — returns `div.markdown` whose innerHTML is a string built ONLY from escaped input plus renderer-authored tags.
   170	
   171	### The pipeline, in exact order (XSS-critical — do not reorder)
   172	
   173	1. **Sanitize sentinels**: `text = String(text).replace(/\u0000/g, "")` — NUL is the placeholder char below; stripping it first makes placeholder collisions impossible.
   174	2. **Escape everything once, before any parsing**: `escapeHtml(s)` replaces, in this order: `&`→`&amp;`, `<`→`&lt;`, `>`→`&gt;`, `"`→`&quot;`, `'`→`&#39;`. From here on the parser only ever sees escaped text; **raw input never reaches innerHTML anywhere**. (All markdown delimiters — `` ` `` `#` `*` `_` `-` `[` `]` `(` `)` and digits — survive escaping untouched, so parsing on escaped text is lossless.)
   175	3. **Line walk** (`escaped.split("\n")`, single pass, block accumulator):
   176	   - line matches `/^```/` → toggle **fence**: open collects following lines verbatim until the closing ```` ``` ````; emit `<pre><code>` + collected lines joined with `\n` + `</code></pre>`. NO inline processing inside fences (content is already escaped — that is its entire treatment). An unclosed fence at EOF emits what was collected.
   177	   - blank line → close the open paragraph/list block.
   178	   - `/^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$/` → heading: `<h1>`–`<h3>` for 1–3 `#`; four or more `#` render as `<h3>` (the visual scale caps at three heading levels inside markdown — five type sizes total, see §9). Heading text goes through `renderInline`.
   179	   - `/^[-*+]\s+(.*)$/` → unordered list item; consecutive items collect into one `<ul>` of `<li>` (each item through `renderInline`). No nesting (minimal renderer).
   180	   - `/^\d{1,9}[.)]\s+(.*)$/` → ordered list item → `<ol>`, same treatment.
   181	   - anything else → paragraph line: each line goes through `renderInline` individually, consecutive lines join with `<br>` inside one `<p>` (planner briefs and proposals rely on single newlines; GFM-comment behavior chosen deliberately — noted in §13).
   182	4. **`renderInline(escapedLine)`**, exact order:
   183	   1. **Code spans first, protected**: replace `/`+`` `([^`]+)` ``+`/g` — each match's inner text is pushed to an array and substituted with `\u0000<index>\u0000`. Code content gets no further processing.
   184	   2. **Links**: `/\[([^\]]+)\]\(([^()\s]+)\)/g` → `<a href="URL">text</a>` only if the URL passes the scheme whitelist: after lowercasing, a URL matching `/^[a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:/` must start with `http:`, `https:`, or `mailto:`; scheme-less (relative, `#/ticket/...`, `?`, `/`) is allowed. A rejected URL leaves the source text as-is (already escaped — inert). The href value is the escaped text (quotes arrive as `&quot;` — cannot break out of the attribute).
   185	   3. **Bold**: `/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g` → `<strong>$1</strong>` (before italic so `**` is not eaten by `*`).
   186	   4. **Italic**: `/\*([^*]+)\*/g` and `/_([^_]+)_/g` → `<em>$1</em>`.
   187	   5. **Restore code spans**: replace each `\u0000<index>\u0000` with `<code>` + stored content + `</code>`.
   188	5. Assemble blocks into one string, create `document.createElement("div")`, `className = "markdown"`, set `innerHTML` to the assembled string, return the element.
   189	
   190	Why this cannot get XSS wrong: (a) the ONE innerHTML assignment receives a string whose every character of user origin passed `escapeHtml` in step 2 — `<`/`>`/quotes cannot exist un-entified; (b) every tag in the string is renderer-authored from a closed set (`p br h1 h2 h3 ul ol li pre code strong em a`); (c) the only attribute ever emitted is `href`, scheme-whitelisted with `javascript:`/`data:`/`vbscript:` (and every other scheme) rejected; (d) placeholders cannot be forged (step 1). No external libs, no other innerHTML in the entire codebase (components use `createElement`/`textContent` only).
   191	
   192	## 5. `assets/components.js` — the D11 primitives (items 1–6, 17)
   193	
   194	Attaches `Planner.components = { appShell, panel, markdownBlock, fieldEditor, chip, entityRow, errorLine }`. All functions return DOM nodes built with `createElement`/`textContent` (never innerHTML). No framework, no near-duplicates: the chip is ONE component with variants; the panel is the ONLY box primitive.
   195	
   196	### 5.1 `appShell() -> { el, content, setReviewBadge(n), setActiveNav(name) }` — D11 item 1
   197	
   198	The one component returning a handle object instead of a bare node — the router needs its content slot and badge/nav setters; flagged in §13.
   199	
   200	```
   201	div.shell
   202	├─ header.shell-nav
   203	│  ├─ span.shell-brand            "planner"
   204	│  └─ nav.shell-links
   205	│     ├─ a.nav-link[data-screen=day][href="#/day"]         "Day"
   206	│     ├─ a.nav-link[data-screen=review][href="#/review"]   "Review"
   207	│     │   └─ span.nav-badge       (hidden; pending count)
   208	│     ├─ a.nav-link[data-screen=board][href="#/board"]     "Board"
   209	│     ├─ a.nav-link[data-screen=sprint][href="#/sprint"]   "Sprint"
   210	│     └─ a.nav-link[data-screen=backlog][href="#/backlog"] "Backlog"
   211	└─ main.shell-content             ← `content`; screens render here
   212	```
   213	
   214	**Nav-set decision:** five links. `#/ticket/<id>` requires an id, so Ticket is not a nav destination — it is reached through entity rows and board cards (`entityRow` hrefs), exactly like SPEC §10.3 "card click opens Ticket". The router still owns the `#/ticket/<id>` route (§6).
   215	
   216	- `setReviewBadge(n)`: `n > 0` → badge `textContent = String(n)`, shown; else `display:none` via a `.hidden` class. Count = `approvals.length` from `GET /api/queues` (tickets/views.py `queues_view` → `{approvals, pickup, overdue}`) — approvals is the Review screen's pending set (§10.2).
   217	- `setActiveNav(name)`: toggles class `active` on the `a[data-screen=name]`; unknown/`ticket` name → no link active.
   218	- Links are plain anchors — navigation is the hash changing; no click handlers, no keyboard layer.
   219	
   220	### 5.2 `panel(title, children) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 2
   221	
   222	`children`: `Node | Node[] | null`.
   223	
   224	```
   225	section.panel
   226	├─ h2.panel-title      textContent = title
   227	└─ div.panel-body      ← children appended
   228	```
   229	
   230	### 5.3 `markdownBlock(text) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 3
   231	
   232	Empty/whitespace `text` → `div.quiet-line` with textContent `(none)` (one quiet line, matching the server's copy-text convention; never instructional). Else `Planner.markdown.render(text)` with `classList.add("markdown-block")`.
   233	
   234	### 5.4 `fieldEditor(value, onSave) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 4
   235	
   236	```
   237	div.field-editor
   238	├─ textarea.field-editor-input    rows=8, value = value || ""
   239	└─ div.field-editor-actions
   240	   ├─ (div.error-line — inserted here on failure, replaced per attempt)
   241	   └─ button.button.button--primary   "Save"
   242	```
   243	Save click: remove any prior `.error-line` in actions; `button.disabled = true`; `Promise.resolve(onSave(textarea.value))` → on rejection `actions.prepend(errorLine(err))` (item 17 inline near the failed action); `finally` re-enable the button. No optimistic UI — after a successful save the WS event invalidates and the screen refetches.
   244	
   245	### 5.5 `chip(variant, value, opts) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 5 (ONE badge, variants)
   246	
   247	Returns `span.chip.chip--<class>` with `data-value` where noted. `opts` only meaningful for `deadline`.
   248	
   249	| variant | value | classes | text | background | text color | border color |
   250	|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
   251	| `priority` | `"P0"` | `chip--priority chip--p0` | `P0` | `--accent-surface` | `--accent-text` | `--accent-bright` |
   252	| `priority` | `"P1"` | `chip--priority chip--p1` | `P1` | `--surface-overlay` | `--text-strong` | `--text-muted` |
   253	| `priority` | `"P2"` | `chip--priority chip--p2` | `P2` | `--surface-overlay` | `--text-default` | `--text-faint` |
   254	| `priority` | `"P3"` | `chip--priority chip--p3` | `P3` | transparent | `--text-muted` | `--text-faint` |
   255	| `state` | any ticket state / item status string | `chip--state` + `data-value` | value with `_`→space | `--surface-overlay` | `--text-default` (`done`/`dropped` → `--text-muted` via `[data-value]`) | transparent |
   256	| `project` | project string | `chip--project` | value | transparent | `--text-muted` | `--text-faint` |
   257	| `deadline` | ISO date | `chip--deadline` (+ `chip--overdue` when `opts.overdue`) | value | transparent / overdue: `--accent-surface` | `--text-muted` / overdue: `--accent-bright` | `--text-faint` / overdue: `--accent-bright` |
   258	| `pending-proposal` | — | `chip--pending-proposal` | `proposal pending` | `--accent-surface` | `--accent-text` | `--accent-bright` |
   259	| `running-claim` | — | `chip--running-claim` | `● running` | `--surface-overlay` | `--text-strong` | `--text-muted` |
   260	| `blockers-cleared` | — | `chip--blockers-cleared` | `blockers cleared` | transparent | `--text-strong` | `--text-muted` |
   261	| `auto-blocked` | — | `chip--auto-blocked` | `auto-blocked` | `--accent-surface` | `--accent-text` | `--accent-bright` |
   262	| `frozen` | — | `chip--frozen` | `frozen` | transparent | `--text-faint` | `--text-faint` |
   263	
   264	The mapping IS the one-accent rule made mechanical: accent styling appears exactly where a human is needed (P0, pending-proposal, auto-blocked, overdue); activity and good news stay achromatic at graded emphasis. Marker labels are fixed in a small internal map; `value` is ignored for markers.
   265	
   266	### 5.6 `entityRow({title, href, chips}) -> HTMLAnchorElement` — D11 item 6
   267	
   268	```
   269	a.entity-row[href]            e.g. "#/ticket/t_abc"
   270	├─ span.entity-row-title      textContent = title
   271	└─ span.entity-row-chips      ← chip nodes appended
   272	```
   273	A native anchor: click-through is a hash change, refresh/middle-click/back all work — state lives in the URL only.
   274	
   275	### 5.7 `errorLine(err) -> HTMLElement` — D11 item 17
   276	
   277	```
   278	div.error-line
   279	├─ span.error-code       textContent = (err && err.code) || "error"
   280	└─ span.error-message    textContent = (err && err.message) || "request failed"
   281	```
   282	Consumes exactly what `fetchJson` rejections carry (the PlannerError envelope passthrough). Rendered inline near the failed action by callers; never a toast/modal layer.
   283	
   284	## 6. `assets/app.js` — router, registry, boot
   285	
   286	Attaches `Planner.registerScreen`; owns the shell instance and boot. Internal state: `lastHash` (entrance detection only — the route itself is always re-derived from `location.hash`).
   287	
   288	### Screen registry
   289	
   290	- `Planner.registerScreen(name, render)` — `render(root, params)`; stored in an internal map. Overwrite-wins: T15–T17's scripts (which will be appended to the shell AFTER app.js) re-register the real screens over the placeholders before `DOMContentLoaded` fires. **This is the entire surface the router exposes** — no navigate(), no route events, no current-route getter; screens navigate by anchors and read `params`.
   291	- T14 registers six placeholders: for each of `day review board ticket sprint backlog`, `render(root)` appends one `div.quiet-line` with textContent `not built yet`. One quiet line — not instructional.
   292	
   293	### Router
   294	
   295	`route()`:
   296	1. `hash = location.hash`; if `""` or `"#"` → `location.replace(Planner.config.ROUTES.day)` and return (the rewrite fires `hashchange`, which re-enters — no double render).
   297	2. `segments = hash.slice(1).split("/").filter(Boolean)` → `["day"]` … `["ticket","t_x"]`.
   298	3. `name = segments[0]`; `params = {}`; `name === "ticket"` → `params.id = segments[1]` (missing id ⇒ unknown route).
   299	4. Unknown name/shape → `shell.setActiveNav(null)`; content gets one `div.quiet-line` `no such screen`; return.
   300	5. `shell.setActiveNav(name)`; `entrance = (hash !== lastHash)`; `lastHash = hash`.
   301	6. `shell.content.replaceChildren(screenDiv)` where `screenDiv = div.screen` (+ class `screen-enter` only when `entrance`); call `registry[name].render(screenDiv, params)`.
   302	
   303	Entrance class only on actual navigation: WS-flush re-renders (same hash) repaint without re-animating — motion marks arrival, not churn.
   304	
   305	### Boot sequence (on `DOMContentLoaded`)
   306	
   307	1. `shell = Planner.components.appShell()`; mount into `#app`.
   308	2. `Planner.api.fetchJson(Planner.config.API.META)` → `debounceMs = meta.ui_debounce_ms`; on failure fall back to `DEBOUNCE_MS_DEFAULT` (one `console.debug`, boot continues — the server just served the page; a meta hiccup must not blank the app).
   309	3. `Planner.bus.onInvalidate(onFlush)`; `Planner.bus.start(debounceMs)` — `onFlush` = `refreshBadge(); route();`. The bus has exactly ONE subscriber; "single notification to the active screen's refetch callback" is structural.
   310	4. `window.addEventListener("hashchange", route)`; then `route()` (which performs the `#/day` default rewrite if needed).
   311	5. `refreshBadge()`: `fetchJson(API.QUEUES)` → `shell.setReviewBadge(res.approvals.length)`; failure → one `console.debug`, badge untouched. Called at boot and on every flush (badge is shell chrome riding the same single flush — not a second subscriber).
   312	
   313	Refresh-restores-state holds by construction: every render derives from `location.hash` + fresh fetches; zero state outlives the URL.
   314	
   315	## 7. `src/planner/core/server.py` — the `_SHELL` rewrite (and nothing else)
   316	
   317	Replace the `_SHELL` constant (currently line 45) with exactly:
   318	
   319	```python
   320	_SHELL = """<!doctype html>
   321	<html lang="en">
   322	<head>
   323	<meta charset="utf-8">
   324	<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1">
   325	<meta name="color-scheme" content="dark">
   326	<title>planner</title>
   327	<link rel="stylesheet" href="/assets/tokens.css">
   328	<link rel="stylesheet" href="/assets/app.css">
   329	</head>
   330	<body>
   331	<div id="app"></div>
   332	<script src="/assets/config.js"></script>
   333	<script src="/assets/api.js"></script>
   334	<script src="/assets/markdown.js"></script>
   335	<script src="/assets/components.js"></script>
   336	<script src="/assets/app.js"></script>
   337	</body>
   338	</html>
   339	"""
   340	```
   341	
   342	Dependency order: tokens before app.css (tokens must be defined when component rules resolve), config → api → markdown → components → app (each later file reads namespaces the earlier ones attached; app.js boots last). The `GET /` handler body (`return _SHELL`) is already correct and stays; **every other byte of server.py is untouched** — assert, lifespan, error handler, router loop, meta, events_ws, test router, StaticFiles mount.
   343	
   344	## 8. `assets/app.css` — component styling, tokens only
   345	
   346	Every rule uses `var(--token)` per §0.4. Selector inventory (must match §5's class names exactly):
   347	
   348	- **Base**: `* { box-sizing: border-box }`; `body { margin: 0; background: var(--surface-base); color: var(--text-default); font-family: var(--font-ui); font-size: var(--type-md); line-height: 1.5; }`; `button, textarea { font: inherit; }`; `:focus-visible { outline: var(--border-strong) solid var(--accent-bright); outline-offset: var(--border-hairline); }`.
   349	- **Shell**: `.shell { min-height: 100vh; }` · `.shell-nav { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-5); padding: var(--space-3) var(--space-5); background: var(--surface-raised); border-bottom: var(--border-hairline) solid var(--surface-overlay); }` · `.shell-brand { font-size: var(--type-sm); font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-strong); }` · `.shell-links { display: flex; gap: var(--space-2); }` · `.nav-link { color: var(--text-muted); font-size: var(--type-sm); text-decoration: none; padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-2); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); transition: color var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-ease), background var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-ease); }` · `.nav-link:hover { color: var(--text-strong); }` · `.nav-link.active { color: var(--text-strong); background: var(--surface-overlay); }` · `.nav-badge { font-size: var(--type-xs); background: var(--accent-surface); color: var(--accent-text); border: var(--border-hairline) solid var(--accent-bright); border-radius: var(--radius-pill); padding: 0 var(--space-2); margin-left: var(--space-1); }` · `.nav-badge.hidden { display: none; }` · `.shell-content { padding: var(--space-5); }`.
   350	- **Entrance**: `.screen-enter { animation: screen-enter var(--motion-slow) var(--motion-ease); }` with `@keyframes screen-enter { from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(var(--space-2)); } to { opacity: 1; transform: none; } }` — the one animation; ease-out, no bounce.
   351	- **Panel**: `.panel { background: var(--surface-raised); border: var(--border-hairline) solid var(--surface-overlay); border-radius: var(--radius-md); margin-bottom: var(--space-4); }` · `.panel-title { margin: 0; padding: var(--space-3) var(--space-4); font-size: var(--type-lg); font-weight: 600; color: var(--text-strong); border-bottom: var(--border-hairline) solid var(--surface-overlay); }` · `.panel-body { padding: var(--space-4); }`.
   352	- **Markdown**: `.markdown p { margin: 0 0 var(--space-3); }` · `.markdown :last-child { margin-bottom: 0; }` · `.markdown h1 { font-size: var(--type-lg); }` · `.markdown h2 { font-size: var(--type-md); font-weight: 600; }` · `.markdown h3 { font-size: var(--type-sm); font-weight: 600; }` (all three: `color: var(--text-strong); margin: var(--space-4) 0 var(--space-2);`) · `.markdown code { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: var(--type-sm); background: var(--surface-sunken); padding: 0 var(--space-1); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); }` · `.markdown pre { background: var(--surface-sunken); padding: var(--space-3); border-radius: var(--radius-md); overflow-x: auto; }` · `.markdown pre code { background: none; padding: 0; }` · `.markdown ul, .markdown ol { margin: 0 0 var(--space-3); padding-left: var(--space-5); }` · `.markdown a { color: var(--accent-bright); }`.
   353	- **Field editor**: `.field-editor { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-2); }` · `.field-editor-input { width: 100%; background: var(--surface-sunken); color: var(--text-default); border: var(--border-hairline) solid var(--text-faint); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: var(--type-sm); line-height: 1.5; resize: vertical; }` · `.field-editor-input:focus { border-color: var(--accent-bright); outline: none; }` · `.field-editor-actions { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: flex-end; gap: var(--space-2); }`.
   354	- **Buttons** (shared, used by Save now, reused by screens later — not a new component, a style class): `.button { background: var(--surface-overlay); color: var(--text-strong); border: var(--border-hairline) solid var(--text-faint); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); padding: var(--space-1) var(--space-3); font-size: var(--type-sm); cursor: pointer; transition: background var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-ease), border-color var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-ease); }` · `.button--primary { background: var(--accent-surface); color: var(--accent-text); border-color: var(--accent-bright); }` · `.button:disabled { opacity: 0.5; cursor: default; }`.
   355	- **Chips**: `.chip { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-1); font-size: var(--type-xs); font-weight: 500; padding: 0 var(--space-2); border-radius: var(--radius-pill); border: var(--border-hairline) solid transparent; background: var(--surface-overlay); color: var(--text-default); white-space: nowrap; line-height: 1.6; }` plus the variant rules exactly per the §5.5 table (`.chip--p0`…`.chip--p3`, `.chip--state[data-value="done"], .chip--state[data-value="dropped"] { color: var(--text-muted); }`, `.chip--project`, `.chip--deadline`, `.chip--overdue`, `.chip--pending-proposal`, `.chip--running-claim`, `.chip--blockers-cleared`, `.chip--auto-blocked`, `.chip--frozen`).
   356	- **Entity row**: `.entity-row { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: var(--space-3); padding: var(--space-2) var(--space-3); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); text-decoration: none; color: var(--text-default); transition: background var(--motion-fast) var(--motion-ease); }` · `.entity-row:hover { background: var(--surface-overlay); }` · `.entity-row-title { font-size: var(--type-md); }` · `.entity-row:hover .entity-row-title { color: var(--text-strong); }` · `.entity-row-chips { display: flex; gap: var(--space-1); flex-shrink: 0; }`.
   357	- **Error line**: `.error-line { display: flex; align-items: baseline; gap: var(--space-2); font-size: var(--type-sm); border-left: var(--border-strong) solid var(--accent-bright); padding-left: var(--space-2); }` · `.error-code { font-family: var(--font-mono); font-size: var(--type-xs); color: var(--accent-text); background: var(--accent-surface); padding: 0 var(--space-1); border-radius: var(--radius-sm); }` · `.error-message { color: var(--text-default); }`.
   358	- **Quiet line** (empty states, placeholders, unknown route): `.quiet-line { color: var(--text-muted); font-size: var(--type-sm); }`.

   360	No max-width/layout literals: the shell is fluid with token padding; content measure is a screen-level concern for T15–T17.

   362	## 9. `orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/smoke.py` — the scripted gate
   363	
   364	Follows the T10 smoke conventions (`ok NN` prints, `SMOKE PASS`, nonzero on any failure, full cleanup in `finally`), but boots **in-process uvicorn** and drives **real headless chromium** (Playwright 1.61.0 + chromium are installed in `.venv` — verified).
   365	
   366	**Event-append endpoint choice: `POST /api/ideas`.** Justified from the code read: `sprints/api.py` `create_idea` → `_create_idea` (lines 80–94) performs exactly one `INSERT INTO ideas` plus exactly one `append_event(..., EventKind.idea_created, ...)` inside one `txn` — one HTTP call ⇒ exactly one new `events` row, no auth headers, no preconditions, no side-band events (unlike ticket creation, which also writes grant/state artifacts). On a fresh DB the event ids are deterministic: three idea posts ⇒ cursor 3.
   367	
   368	Steps (numbered `ok` checks):
   369	
   370	1. `REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[3]`; `os.chdir(REPO)` — `create_app` mounts `StaticFiles(directory="assets")` cwd-relative.
   371	2. Temp dir; pick a free port (T10's `free_or` helper). Build an **explicit env dict** (never mutate `os.environ`) and `config = load_config(path=None, env=env)` with: `PLAN_TEST_MODE=1`, `PLAN_FAKE_NOW=2026-07-04T12:00:00`, `PLAN_DB_PATH=<tmp>/planning.db`, `PLAN_PORT=<port>`, `PLAN_LOGS_DIR=<tmp>/logs`, `PLAN_DISPATCHER_LOCK_PATH=<tmp>/dispatcher.lock`, and `PLAN_WS_POLL_MS=50` (config.py:147 — tightens batch cadence so the coalescing proof is timing-robust; ui_debounce_ms stays the default 250 so check (b) asserts the real value).
   372	3. Mirror `cli/main.py serve` wiring (lines 59–71): `connect` + `create_schema` on the temp DB; `build_clock(config)`; `build_adapters(config)` (test mode ⇒ fakes); `conn_factory` closure; `app = create_app(...)`.
   373	4. `server = uvicorn.Server(uvicorn.Config(app, host="127.0.0.1", port=port, log_level="warning"))`; run `server.run` in a daemon `threading.Thread` (uvicorn skips signal handlers off the main thread). Readiness: T10's `wait_ready` loop on `GET /api/meta`. → `ok` "server up".
   374	5. **(a) Shell references, in order**: `GET /` → 200, `text/html`; walk `body.index(...)` cursor-style over `"/assets/tokens.css"`, `"/assets/app.css"`, `"/assets/config.js"`, `"/assets/api.js"`, `"/assets/markdown.js"`, `"/assets/components.js"`, `"/assets/app.js"` asserting strictly increasing positions (proves presence AND order: both CSS, then the five scripts). Then `GET /assets/<each of the 7>` → 200 (mount actually serves them; catches filename typos). → `ok`.

   414	## 12. Risks / notes for the orchestrator's sense-check
   415	
   416	1. **Type tokens added** (`--type-xs…xl`, `--font-ui`, `--font-mono`) — the sanctioned evidence-of-need case; three-line argument in §1. If overruled, app.css cannot satisfy "zero literal sizes".
   417	2. **Accent changed blue → amber** with a fixed meaning ("needs the human"); surfaces de-blued to keep the accent the only hue. Pure personality call, reversible by editing tokens.css alone — which is the design system working as specced.
   418	3. **Nav set**: five links (Day, Review+badge, Board, Sprint, Backlog); Ticket routed but reached only via rows/cards since `#/ticket/<id>` needs an id. SPEC §10.3's "card click opens Ticket" supports this.
   419	4. **`appShell` returns a handle object** (`{el, content, setReviewBadge, setActiveNav}`), not a bare node — the router needs the slot and setters; the other six components return plain nodes.
   420	5. **Console hook shape**: `window.__plannerDebug = {flushes, wsOpens, cursor}` + exactly one `console.debug("[planner] flush n")` per bus flush and one per WS open. One deliberate extra global beside `Planner`; sanctioned by the ticket's "observable via console hook".
   421	6. **Attention variants share one look**: P0, pending-proposal, auto-blocked, overdue all wear the accent (labels differentiate). Deliberate consequence of ONE accent — visual language stays honest instead of inventing semantic colors (which would be a new token category without sanction).
   422	7. **Markdown: single newline → `<br>`** inside paragraphs (planner briefs/proposals are chat-adjacent text; strict-markdown space-joining would silently mangle them). Headings cap at `h3` (4+ `#` render as h3) to stay inside five type sizes.
   423	8. **Unknown route** → one quiet line `no such screen` (no redirect — the URL stays honest). Empty markdown → `(none)`, matching the server's copy-text convention.
   424	9. **Meta fetch failure** at boot falls back to the 250ms default from config.js and boots anyway — a meta hiccup must not blank an already-served page. Not speculative resilience: it is the ticket's stated purpose for the config.js default.
   425	10. **Smoke tempo**: server runs with `PLAN_WS_POLL_MS=50` so the two-event coalescing proof has ~230ms of slack; `ui_debounce_ms` stays 250 (asserted). Residual flake risk only if two localhost POSTs straddle >200ms — accepted; if it ever flakes, the fallback is widening the sleep, not weakening the exactly-one assertion. Reconnect/backoff is implemented per §3 but not smoke-tested (needs a server bounce) — Codex review should eyeball that path.
   426	11. **T15–T17 seam**: their `<script>` tags will be appended to `_SHELL` after `app.js`; classic scripts execute in order before `DOMContentLoaded`, so `registerScreen` overwrite-wins replaces the placeholders before boot routes. Router exposes `registerScreen(name, render(root, params))` and nothing else.
   427	12. **Audio seam (§14)**: no chat code in T14; the bus and components are input-source-agnostic; nothing here assumes typed text anywhere. The chat panel (D11 item 10) lands later behind the gateway adapter.

/bin/zsh -lc 'nl -ba src/planner/core/ws.py' in /Users/khushaljagota/.hermes/planning-v2
 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	"""§9 event tailer. WS /api/events?since=<id> tails the events table and pushes
     2	{events, cursor} batches — an invalidation signal, non-empty batches only, no
     3	server-side reconciliation. One sqlite connection per WS connection, created and
     4	used on the event-loop thread (sqlite3 same-thread) and closed when the coroutine
     5	ends, including on cancellation.
     6	
     7	A per-connection watcher task drains the receive side: it awaits websocket.receive()
     8	until the ASGI "websocket.disconnect" arrives — sent both when the client closes and
     9	when uvicorn tears the connection down on shutdown. The poll loop wakes on that signal
    10	(instead of sleeping blind), so a parked idle tailer exits promptly on Ctrl-C and never
    11	holds graceful shutdown open, and a silently-gone client is noticed without a send.
    12	
    13	FastAPI appears here because this is part of the server shell (the server.py family)."""
    14	
    15	from __future__ import annotations
    16	
    17	import asyncio
    18	import sqlite3
    19	from collections.abc import Callable
    20	
    21	from fastapi import WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
    22	
    23	from planner.core.contracts import EventRow, JsonDict
    24	from planner.core.events import read_events_since
    25	
    26	
    27	def _event_json(row: EventRow) -> JsonDict:
    28	    return {
    29	        "id": row.id,
    30	        "entity_id": row.entity_id,
    31	        "kind": row.kind,
    32	        "payload": row.payload,
    33	        "created_at": row.created_at,
    34	    }
    35	
    36	
    37	async def _watch_disconnect(websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
    38	    while True:
    39	        message = await websocket.receive()
    40	        if message["type"] == "websocket.disconnect":
    41	            return
    42	
    43	
    44	async def tail_events(
    45	    websocket: WebSocket,
    46	    since: int,
    47	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    48	    ws_poll_ms: int,
    49	    events_read_limit: int,
    50	) -> None:
    51	    await websocket.accept()
    52	    conn = conn_factory()
    53	    watcher = asyncio.create_task(_watch_disconnect(websocket))
    54	    try:
    55	        cursor = since
    56	        while not watcher.done():
    57	            batch = read_events_since(conn, cursor, events_read_limit)
    58	            if batch:
    59	                cursor = batch[-1].id
    60	                await websocket.send_json(
    61	                    {"events": [_event_json(e) for e in batch], "cursor": cursor}
    62	                )
    63	                if len(batch) == events_read_limit:
    64	                    continue
    65	            else:
    66	                await asyncio.wait({watcher}, timeout=ws_poll_ms / 1000)
    67	    except WebSocketDisconnect:
    68	        pass
    69	    finally:
    70	        watcher.cancel()
    71	        try:
    72	            await asyncio.wait({watcher})
    73	        finally:
    74	            conn.close()

    79	
    80	def _create_idea(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, title: str, body: str, project: Project | None,
    81	                 now: int) -> JsonDict:
    82	    if not title:
    83	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "idea title is required", {})
    84	    idea_id = new_id(ID_PREFIXES["idea"])
    85	    with txn(conn):
    86	        conn.execute(
    87	            "INSERT INTO ideas (id, title, body, project, created_at, updated_at) "
    88	            "VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
    89	            (idea_id, title, body, project.value if project is not None else None, now, now),
    90	        )
    91	        append_event(conn, idea_id, EventKind.idea_created, {"title": title, "source": "api"}, now)
    92	    row = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM ideas WHERE id = ?", (idea_id,)).fetchone()
    93	    assert row is not None
    94	    return sprints_views.idea_json(row)
    95	
    96	
    97	def _set_sprint_dates(conn: sqlite3.Connection, sprint_id: str, *, date_start: str | None,
    98	                      date_end: str | None, clock: Clock) -> None:
    99	    sprint = sprints_data.read_sprint(conn, sprint_id)
   100	    new_start = date_start if date_start is not None else sprint.date_start
   101	    new_end = date_end if date_end is not None else sprint.date_end
   102	    for label, value in (("date_start", new_start), ("date_end", new_end)):
   103	        try:
   104	            date.fromisoformat(value)
   105	        except ValueError as exc:
   106	            raise PlannerError(
   107	                ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {label}", {label: value}
   108	            ) from exc
   109	    if new_start > new_end:
   110	        raise PlannerError(
   111	            ErrorCode.validation,
   112	            "date_start must not be after date_end",
   113	            {"date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   114	        )
   115	    others = [
   116	        DateRange(id=str(r["id"]), date_start=str(r["date_start"]), date_end=str(r["date_end"]))
   117	        for r in conn.execute(
   118	            "SELECT id, date_start, date_end FROM sprints WHERE id != ?", (sprint_id,)
   119	        ).fetchall()
   120	    ]
   121	    conflict = find_overlap(new_start, new_end, others)
   122	    if conflict is not None:
   123	        raise PlannerError(
   124	            ErrorCode.sprint_overlap,
   125	            "sprint dates overlap",
   126	            {"conflict_id": conflict, "date_start": new_start, "date_end": new_end},
   127	        )
   128	    now = clock.now_unix()
   129	    with txn(conn):
   130	        conn.execute(
   131	            "UPDATE sprints SET date_start = ?, date_end = ?, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?",
   132	            (new_start, new_end, now, sprint_id),
   133	        )
   134	        if new_start != sprint.date_start:
   135	            append_event(
   136	                conn,
   137	                sprint_id,
   138	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   139	                {"field": "date_start", "from": sprint.date_start, "to": new_start},
   140	                now,
   141	            )
   142	        if new_end != sprint.date_end:
   143	            append_event(
   144	                conn,
   145	                sprint_id,
   146	                EventKind.sprint_updated,
   147	                {"field": "date_end", "from": sprint.date_end, "to": new_end},
   148	                now,
   149	            )
   150	
   151	
   152	def _marshal_item_deadline(raw: object) -> None:
   153	    if raw is None:
   154	        return
   155	    if not isinstance(raw, str):
   156	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw})
   157	    try:
   158	        date.fromisoformat(raw)
   159	    except ValueError as exc:
   160	        raise PlannerError(
   161	            ErrorCode.validation, "invalid deadline", {"deadline": raw}
   162	        ) from exc

   169	async def create_item(body: CreateItemBody, conn: DbConn, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   170	    if body.project is None:
   171	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "project is required")
   172	    project = parse_enum(Project, body.project, "project")

   205	
   206	
   207	@router.patch("/items/{item_id}")
   208	async def patch_item(item_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   209	                     clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   210	    recognized = set(_ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS) | {"sprint_id", "status", "blocked_by"}
   211	    for key in body:
   212	        if key not in recognized:
   213	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown item field", {"field": key})
   214	    if not body:
   215	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no item fields to update", {})
   216	    if "blocked_by" in body and "status" not in body:
   217	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "blocked_by requires status", {})
   218	    for field in _ITEM_PLAIN_FIELDS:
   219	        if field in body:
   220	            if field == "deadline":
   221	                _marshal_item_deadline(body["deadline"])
   222	            sprints_data.update_item_field(conn, item_id, field, body[field], clock=clk)
   223	    if "sprint_id" in body:
   224	        sprints_data.assign_item_sprint(conn, item_id, body["sprint_id"], clock=clk)
   225	    if "status" in body:
   226	        to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body["status"], "status")
   227	        sprints_data.transition_item_status(
   228	            conn, item_id, to_status, clock=clk, by_agent=not ctx.is_human,
   229	            blocked_by=body.get("blocked_by"),
   230	        )
   231	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)
   232	
   233	
   234	@router.post("/items/{item_id}/propose-status")
   235	async def propose_item_status(item_id: str, body: ProposeStatusBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx,
   236	                              clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   237	    to_status = parse_enum(ItemStatus, body.to, "status")
   238	    sprints_data.propose_item_status(
   239	        conn, item_id, to_status, note=body.note, proposed_by=ctx.actor, clock=clk
   240	    )
   241	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)

   246	    reject_agents(ctx)
   247	    sprints_data.accept_item_status(conn, item_id, clock=clk, resolved_by="human")
   248	    return sprints_views.item_detail(conn, item_id)

   255	async def create_sprint(body: CreateSprintBody, conn: DbConn, ctx: Ctx, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   256	    reject_agents(ctx)
   257	    for label, raw in (("date_start", body.date_start), ("date_end", body.date_end)):
   258	        try:
   259	            date.fromisoformat(raw)
   260	        except ValueError:
   261	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid {label}", {label: raw}) from None

   286	@router.patch("/sprints/{sprint_id}")
   287	async def patch_sprint(sprint_id: str, body: dict[str, Any], conn: DbConn, clk: Clk) -> JsonDict:
   288	    recognized = set(_SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS) | {"date_start", "date_end"}
   289	    for key in body:
   290	        if key not in recognized:
   291	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "unknown sprint field", {"field": key})
   292	    if not body:
   293	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, "no sprint fields to update", {})
   294	    for field in _SPRINT_TEXT_FIELDS:
   295	        if field in body:
   296	            sprints_data.update_sprint_field(conn, sprint_id, field, body[field], clock=clk)
   297	    if "date_start" in body or "date_end" in body:
   298	        _set_sprint_dates(
   299	            conn, sprint_id, date_start=body.get("date_start"), date_end=body.get("date_end"),
   300	            clock=clk,
   301	        )
   302	    return sprints_views.sprint_json(sprints_data.read_sprint(conn, sprint_id))

     2	static assets and the app shell, exposes GET /api/meta and the event WebSocket,
     3	and — only in test mode — the /api/test/* control endpoints. The resolution

     5	and the wiring are fixed.

     7	This module and the domain api.py files are the only places FastAPI/pydantic

    40	    ErrorCode.not_found: 404,
    41	    ErrorCode.stale_claim: 409,
    42	    ErrorCode.gateway_offline: 503,
    43	}
    44

    46	
    47	
    48	def http_status_for(code: ErrorCode) -> int:
    49	    return _STATUS_BY_CODE.get(code, 400)
    50	
    51	
    52	def create_app(
    53	    config: Config,
    54	    clock: Clock,
    55	    adapters: Adapters,
    56	    conn_factory: Callable[[], sqlite3.Connection],
    57	) -> FastAPI:
    58	    # The DDL carries the literal 200 title cap; the config value is what write
    59	    # paths enforce. Guard that they agree (amendment 11). Factory time is startup
    60	    # and strictly earlier than any request, so this fires even for TestClient apps
    61	    # that never enter the lifespan.
    62	    assert config.title_max_chars == 200, "title_max_chars must equal the DDL literal (200)"
    63	
    64	    @asynccontextmanager
    65	    async def _lifespan(app_: FastAPI) -> AsyncIterator[None]:
    66	        Path(config.db_path).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    67	        Path(config.logs_dir).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    68	        loops: Any = None
    69	        if not config.test_mode:  # D6: background loops never run in test mode
    70	            try:
    71	                module = importlib.import_module("planner.core.loops")
    72	                start = module.start_background_loops
    73	            except (ImportError, AttributeError):
    74	                _log.warning("planner.core.loops unavailable; running without background loops")
    75	            else:
    76	                loops = start(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory)
    77	        try:
    78	            yield
    79	        finally:
    80	            if loops is not None:
    81	                await loops.stop()
    82	
    83	    app = FastAPI(title="planner", version="2.0.0", lifespan=_lifespan)
    84	    app.state.config = config
    85	    app.state.clock = clock
    86	    app.state.adapters = adapters
    87	    app.state.conn_factory = conn_factory
    88	
    89	    @app.exception_handler(PlannerError)
    90	    async def handle_planner_error(request: Request, exc: PlannerError) -> JSONResponse:
    91	        return JSONResponse(status_code=http_status_for(exc.code), content=exc.to_payload())
    92	
    93	    for domain_router in (
    94	        tickets_router,
    95	        sprints_router,
    96	        days_router,
    97	        dispatch_router,
    98	        seed_router,
    99	        chat_router,
   100	    ):
   101	        app.include_router(domain_router, prefix="/api")
   102	
   103	    @app.get("/api/meta")
   104	    async def meta() -> dict[str, Any]:
   105	        return {
   106	            "ui_debounce_ms": config.ui_debounce_ms,
   107	            "ws_poll_ms": config.ws_poll_ms,
   108	            "test_mode": config.test_mode,
   109	        }
   110	
   111	    @app.websocket("/api/events")
   112	    async def events_ws(websocket: WebSocket, since: int = 0) -> None:
   113	        await tail_events(
   114	            websocket, since, conn_factory, config.ws_poll_ms, config.events_read_limit
   115	        )
   116	
   117	    @app.get("/", response_class=HTMLResponse)
   118	    async def index() -> str:
   119	        return _SHELL
   120	
   121	    if config.test_mode:
   122	        app.include_router(
   123	            build_test_router(config, clock, adapters, conn_factory), prefix="/api"
   124	        )
   125	
   126	    app.mount("/assets", StaticFiles(directory="assets"), name="assets")
   127	    return app

    91	def list_tickets(
    92	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    93	    now: int,
    94	    *,
    95	    state: TicketState | None,
    96	    project: Project | None,
    97	    sprint_id: str | None,
    98	    sprint_item_id: str | None,
    99	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   100	    clauses: list[str] = []
   101	    params: list[str] = []
   102	    if state is not None:
   103	        clauses.append("state = ?")
   104	        params.append(state.value)
   105	    if project is not None:
   106	        clauses.append("project = ?")
   107	        params.append(project.value)
   108	    if sprint_id is not None:
   109	        clauses.append("sprint_id = ?")
   110	        params.append(sprint_id)
   111	    if sprint_item_id is not None:
   112	        clauses.append("sprint_item_id = ?")
   113	        params.append(sprint_item_id)
   114	    where = (" WHERE " + " AND ".join(clauses)) if clauses else ""
   115	    rows = conn.execute(
   116	        "SELECT id FROM tickets" + where + " ORDER BY created_at ASC, id", tuple(params)
   117	    ).fetchall()
   118	    return [ticket_json(tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, str(row["id"])), now) for row in rows]

   121	def ticket_detail(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   122	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   123	    detail = ticket_json(ticket, now)
   124	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   125	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   126	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   127	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   128	    ).fetchall()
   129	    day_rows = conn.execute(
   130	        "SELECT day_id FROM day_tickets WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY day_id ASC", (ticket_id,)
   131	    ).fetchall()
   132	    total = int(
   133	        conn.execute(
   134	            "SELECT COUNT(*) AS n FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ?", (ticket_id,)
   135	        ).fetchone()["n"]
   136	    )
   137	    running = conn.execute(
   138	        "SELECT 1 FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? AND status = 'running' LIMIT 1", (ticket_id,)
   139	    ).fetchone()
   140	    latest = conn.execute(
   141	        "SELECT * FROM runs WHERE ticket_id = ? ORDER BY started_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1",
   142	        (ticket_id,),
   143	    ).fetchone()
   144	    detail.update(
   145	        {
   146	            "blocked": core_links.is_blocked(conn, ticket_id),
   147	            "effective_sprint_id": tickets_data.get_effective_sprint_id(conn, ticket_id),
   148	            "links": [
   149	                {"from_id": str(r["from_id"]), "to_id": str(r["to_id"]), "kind": str(r["kind"])}
   150	                for r in link_rows
   151	            ],
   152	            "day_ids": [str(r["day_id"]) for r in day_rows],
   153	            "run_summary": {
   154	                "total": total,
   155	                "running": running is not None,
   156	                "latest": run_json(latest) if latest is not None else None,
   157	            },
   158	        }
   159	    )
   160	    return detail
   161

   166	    ).fetchall()

   184	def copy_text(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ticket_id: str) -> str:
   185	    ticket = tickets_data.read_ticket(conn, ticket_id)
   186	    fields = ticket.fields
   187	
   188	    def show(value: str | None) -> str:
   189	        return value if value else "(none)"
   190	
   191	    link_rows = conn.execute(
   192	        "SELECT from_id, to_id, kind FROM links WHERE from_id = ? OR to_id = ? "
   193	        "ORDER BY kind, from_id, to_id",
   194	        (ticket_id, ticket_id),
   195	    ).fetchall()
   196	    if link_rows:
   197	        links_block = "\n".join(
   198	            f"- {str(r['kind'])}: {str(r['from_id'])} -> {str(r['to_id'])}" for r in link_rows
   199	        )
   200	    else:
   201	        links_block = "(none)"
   202	    return (
   203	        f"{ticket.title}\n"
   204	        f"state: {ticket.state.value}\n"
   205	        f"priority: {ticket.priority.value}\n"
   206	        f"\n"
   207	        f"success:\n{show(fields.success.value)}\n"
   208	        f"\n"
   209	        f"approach:\n{show(fields.approach.value)}\n"
   210	        f"\n"
   211	        f"plan:\n{show(fields.plan.value)}\n"
   212	        f"\n"
   213	        f"result:\n{show(fields.result.value)}\n"
   214	        f"\n"
   215	        f"recap:\n{show(ticket.recap)}\n"
   216	        f"\n"
   217	        f"links:\n{links_block}\n"
   218	    )

   220	
   221	# --- board (§10.3) -------------------------------------------------------------
   222	
   223	
   224	def board_view(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> JsonDict:
   225	    rows = conn.execute(
   226	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline, project, fields, claim_lock, "
   227	        "claim_expires, created_at FROM tickets WHERE state != 'dropped'"
   228	    ).fetchall()
   229	    by_state: dict[str, list[tuple[tuple[int, int, str, int], JsonDict]]] = {
   230	        s.value: [] for s in STATE_ORDER
   231	    }
   232	    for row in rows:
   233	        state = str(row["state"])
   234	        priority = str(row["priority"])
   235	        deadline = str(row["deadline"]) if row["deadline"] is not None else None
   236	        fields = json.loads(str(row["fields"]))
   237	        claim_lock = str(row["claim_lock"]) if row["claim_lock"] is not None else None
   238	        claim_expires = int(row["claim_expires"]) if row["claim_expires"] is not None else None
   239	        card: JsonDict = {
   240	            "id": str(row["id"]),
   241	            "title": str(row["title"]),
   242	            "priority": priority,
   243	            "deadline": deadline,
   244	            "project": str(row["project"]) if row["project"] is not None else None,
   245	            "has_pending_proposal": gating_field_pending(TicketState(state), fields),
   246	            "has_running_claim": has_active_claim(claim_lock, claim_expires, now),
   247	        }
   248	        sort_key = (
   249	            _prio_rank(priority),
   250	            0 if deadline is not None else 1,
   251	            deadline or "",
   252	            int(row["created_at"]),
   253	        )
   254	        by_state[state].append((sort_key, card))
   255	    columns: list[JsonDict] = []
   256	    for s in STATE_ORDER:
   257	        cards = [card for _, card in sorted(by_state[s.value], key=lambda item: item[0])]
   258	        columns.append({"state": s.value, "cards": cards})
   259	    return {"columns": columns}
   260	
   261	
   262	# --- queues (§4.5) -------------------------------------------------------------
   263	
   264	
   265	def _entity_type(entity_id: str) -> str:
   266	    return "ticket" if entity_id.split("_", 1)[0] == "t" else "item"
   267	
   268	
   269	def _approvals(conn: sqlite3.Connection, item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict]) -> list[JsonDict]:
   270	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   271	        "SELECT id, title, state, fields, updated_at FROM tickets "
   272	        "WHERE state NOT IN ('done','dropped') ORDER BY id"
   273	    ).fetchall()
   274	    ticket_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   275	    ticket_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   276	    ticket_updated: dict[str, int] = {}
   277	    for r in ticket_rows:
   278	        tid = str(r["id"])
   279	        ticket_digest.append(
   280	            {
   281	                "id": tid,
   282	                "state": str(r["state"]),
   283	                "fields": json.loads(str(r["fields"])),
   284	                "updated_at": int(r["updated_at"]),
   285	            }
   286	        )
   287	        ticket_title[tid] = str(r["title"])
   288	        ticket_updated[tid] = int(r["updated_at"])
   289	    item_digest: list[dict[str, object]] = []
   290	    item_title: dict[str, str] = {}
   291	    for r in item_approval_rows:
   292	        iid = str(r["id"])
   293	        item_digest.append({"id": iid, "status_proposal": r["status_proposal"]})
   294	        item_title[iid] = str(r["title"])
   295	    digest = approvals_digest(ticket_digest, item_digest)
   296	    # A5: review entries use the last state_changed->needs_review event time, not the
   297	    # updated_at proxy; the boundary digest keeps its documented proxy.
   298	    for entry in digest:
   299	        if entry["kind"] == "review":
   300	            tid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   301	            row = conn.execute(
   302	                "SELECT created_at FROM events WHERE entity_id = ? AND kind = 'state_changed' "
   303	                "AND json_extract(payload, '$.to') = 'needs_review' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1",
   304	                (tid,),
   305	            ).fetchone()
   306	            entry["waiting_since"] = (
   307	                int(row["created_at"]) if row is not None
   308	                else ticket_updated.get(tid, entry["waiting_since"])
   309	            )
   310	    digest.sort(key=lambda e: e["waiting_since"])
   311	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   312	    for entry in digest:
   313	        eid = str(entry["entity_id"])
   314	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   315	        title = ticket_title.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_title.get(eid)
   316	        result.append(
   317	            {
   318	                "entity_id": eid,
   319	                "entity_type": etype,
   320	                "kind": entry["kind"],
   321	                "title": title,
   322	                "waiting_since": entry["waiting_since"],
   323	            }
   324	        )
   325	    return result
   326	
   327	
   328	def _pickup(conn: sqlite3.Connection, now: int) -> list[JsonDict]:
   329	    candidates = dispatch_data.load_candidates(conn, now)
   330	    eligible = [c for c in candidates if is_eligible(c)]
   331	    eligible.sort(key=ordering_key)
   332	    if not eligible:
   333	        return []
   334	    ids = [c.ticket_id for c in eligible]
   335	    placeholders = ",".join("?" for _ in ids)
   336	    rows = conn.execute(
   337	        f"SELECT id, title FROM tickets WHERE id IN ({placeholders})", tuple(ids)
   338	    ).fetchall()
   339	    title_by_id = {str(r["id"]): str(r["title"]) for r in rows}
   340	    return [
   341	        {
   342	            "ticket_id": c.ticket_id,
   343	            "title": title_by_id.get(c.ticket_id, ""),
   344	            "state": c.state.value,
   345	            "priority": c.priority.value,
   346	            "deadline": c.deadline,
   347	        }
   348	        for c in eligible
   349	    ]
   350	
   351	
   352	def _overdue(
   353	    conn: sqlite3.Connection, today_iso: str, item_overdue_rows: list[JsonDict]
   354	) -> list[JsonDict]:
   355	    ticket_rows = conn.execute(
   356	        "SELECT id, title, state, priority, deadline FROM tickets WHERE deadline IS NOT NULL "
   357	        "ORDER BY deadline ASC, id"
   358	    ).fetchall()
   359	    ticket_dicts: list[dict[str, object]] = [
   360	        {
   361	            "id": str(r["id"]),
   362	            "title": str(r["title"]),
   363	            "state": str(r["state"]),
   364	            "priority": str(r["priority"]),
   365	            "deadline": str(r["deadline"]) if r["deadline"] is not None else None,
   366	        }
   367	        for r in ticket_rows
   368	    ]
   369	    item_dicts: list[dict[str, object]] = [dict(r) for r in item_overdue_rows]
   370	    digest = overdue_list(ticket_dicts, item_dicts, today_iso)
   371	    ticket_deadline = {str(d["id"]): d["deadline"] for d in ticket_dicts}
   372	    item_deadline = {str(d["id"]): d["deadline"] for d in item_dicts}
   373	    result: list[JsonDict] = []
   374	    for entry in digest:
   375	        eid = str(entry["id"])
   376	        etype = _entity_type(eid)
   377	        deadline = ticket_deadline.get(eid) if etype == "ticket" else item_deadline.get(eid)
   378	        result.append(
   379	            {
   380	                "id": eid,
   381	                "entity_type": etype,
   382	                "title": entry["title"],
   383	                "state": entry["state"],
   384	                "priority": entry["priority"],
   385	                "deadline": deadline,
   386	            }
   387	        )
   388	    return result
   389	
   390	
   391	def queues_view(
   392	    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
   393	    now: int,
   394	    today_iso: str,
   395	    item_approval_rows: list[JsonDict],
   396	    item_overdue_rows: list[JsonDict],
   397	) -> JsonDict:
   398	    return {
   399	        "approvals": _approvals(conn, item_approval_rows),
   400	        "pickup": _pickup(conn, now),
   401	        "overdue": _overdue(conn, today_iso, item_overdue_rows),
   402	    }

    57	def _parse_bool(raw: object, key: str) -> bool:
    58	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    59	        return raw
    60	    text = str(raw).strip().lower()
    61	    if text in _TRUE:
    62	        return True
    63	    if text in _FALSE:
    64	        return False
    65	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid boolean for {key}: {raw!r}")
    66	
    67	
    68	def _parse_int(raw: object, key: str) -> int:
    69	    if isinstance(raw, bool):
    70	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")
    71	    if isinstance(raw, int):
    72	        return raw
    73	    if isinstance(raw, str):
    74	        try:
    75	            return int(raw.strip())
    76	        except ValueError as exc:
    77	            raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}") from exc
    78	    raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"invalid integer for {key}: {raw!r}")
    79	
    80	
    81	def _read_yaml(path: str | None) -> dict[str, object]:
    82	    resolved = Path(path) if path is not None else Path(DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH)
    83	    if not resolved.exists():
    84	        return {}
    85	    loaded = yaml.safe_load(resolved.read_text())
    86	    if loaded is None:
    87	        return {}
    88	    if not isinstance(loaded, dict):
    89	        raise PlannerError(ErrorCode.validation, f"config root must be a mapping: {resolved}")
    90	    return loaded

   101	    return str(value)

     1	"""Structured errors. Every domain rejection raises PlannerError with a stable
     2	code; the API renders it as {"error": {code, message, detail}} and the CLI keys
     3	its exit code off the presence of that envelope."""
     4	
     5	from __future__ import annotations
     6	
     7	from enum import StrEnum
     8	
     9	from planner.core.contracts import JsonDict
    10	
    11	
    12	class ErrorCode(StrEnum):
    13	    frozen_write = "frozen_write"                  # §5 kickoff/review writes after freeze
    14	    at_cap_stop = "at_cap_stop"                    # §4.3 agent proposal at ceiling with stop
    15	    grant_missing = "grant_missing"                # §4.4.7 accept without the full pair
    16	    grant_invalid = "grant_invalid"                # next_ceiling before the new state / unknown
    17	    stale_claim = "stale_claim"                    # §7.6 stale/foreign claim; detail names it
    18	    recap_too_early = "recap_too_early"            # §3.3 recap write at needs_success
    19	    title_too_long = "title_too_long"              # §3.3 > title_max_chars
    20	    sprint_overlap = "sprint_overlap"              # §3.1 overlapping date ranges
    21	    link_cycle = "link_cycle"                      # §3.6 blocks/parent_child transitive cycle
    22	    link_invalid = "link_invalid"                  # self-link, second belongs_to, bad endpoints
    23	    sprint_derived = "sprint_derived"              # §3.3 sprint_id write on a parented ticket
    24	    item_transition_forbidden = "item_transition_forbidden"  # §3.2 agent direct done/deferred
    25	    agent_forbidden = "agent_forbidden"            # claim/agent request hits a human-only action
    26	    gateway_offline = "gateway_offline"            # §11
    27	    db_not_empty = "db_not_empty"                  # §12 seed --demo on a non-empty DB
    28	    not_found = "not_found"
    29	    validation = "validation"                      # generic input validation
    30	
    31	
    32	class PlannerError(Exception):
    33	    def __init__(self, code: ErrorCode, message: str, detail: JsonDict | None = None) -> None:
    34	        super().__init__(message)
    35	        self.code = code
    36	        self.message = message
    37	        self.detail: JsonDict = detail if detail is not None else {}
    38	
    39	    def to_payload(self) -> JsonDict:
    40	        return {"error": {"code": self.code.value, "message": self.message, "detail": self.detail}}

orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/plan.md:6:**Approach.** Five classic scripts compose under one namespace object `window.Planner` (each file is an IIFE that does `window.Planner = window.Planner || {}` and attaches exactly one sub-namespace), so seven asset files load in `<script src>` order with no bundler and every file passes `node --check`. The data layer is deliberately stateless: `api.js` holds only a WS cursor, a debounce timer, a backoff counter, and one registered flush callback; every flush makes `app.js` re-run the active screen's `render(root, params)`, which refetches full JSON — WS payloads are never read beyond `msg.cursor` (SPEC §9 audit target). Rendering primitives are the seven D11 components, all styled in `app.css` exclusively through `var(--token)`; the whole personality (restrained neutral-dark surfaces, one amber accent meaning "needs the human", five type sizes, ease-out motion) lives in `tokens.css`. The server change is confined to the `_SHELL` string. The smoke gate boots in-process uvicorn in test mode and drives real headless chromium to prove: shell references in order, meta keys, default route + placeholders, and the event-append → one-debounced-flush invariant (single event ⇒ exactly 1 flush; two rapid events ⇒ exactly 1 flush).
orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/plan.md:12:1. Every JS file passes `node --check`: classic scripts, **no** `import`/`export`/module syntax, no top-level `await`. Globals only via `window.Planner` (plus the one sanctioned debug hook `window.__plannerDebug`, see §5).

orchestration/tickets/T14-ui-foundation/plan-review-raw.txt:488:     6	**Approach.** Five classic scripts compose under one namespace object `window.Planner` (each file is an IIFE that does `window.Planner = window.Planner || {}` and attaches exactly one sub-namespace), so seven asset files load in `<script src>` order with no bundler and every file passes `node --check`. The data layer is deliberately stateless: `api.js` holds only a WS cursor, a debounce timer, a backoff counter, and one registered flush callback; every flush makes `app.js` re-run the active screen's `render(root, params)`, which refetches full JSON — WS payloads are never read beyond `msg.cursor` (SPEC §9 audit target). Rendering primitives are the seven D11 components, all styled in `app.css` exclusively through `var(--token)`; the whole personality (restrained neutral-dark surfaces, one amber accent meaning "needs the human", five type sizes, ease-out motion) lives in `tokens.css`. The server change is confined to the `_SHELL` string. The smoke gate boots in-process uvicorn in test mode and drives real headless chromium to prove: shell references in order, meta keys, default route + placeholders, and the event-append → one-debounced-flush invariant (single event ⇒ exactly 1 flush; two rapid events ⇒ exactly 1 flush).

SPEC.md:11:A database-backed personal planning system replacing a markdown-file system. It manages sprints, sprint items, tickets, and planning days for a single user (Khushal), and lets AI agents take tickets through a shaping-and-execution pipeline under explicit per-ticket permission ceilings. It runs entirely on the local machine.
SPEC.md:13:The system is: one SQLite database; one Python server process (JSON API + web UI + WebSocket event feed + embedded dispatcher + boundary scheduler); one CLI used by agents (the human operates through the UI, never the CLI); a set of skill documents as artifacts. Agents never mutate canonical state directly — they write proposals; code resolves them.
SPEC.md:19:- Frontend: no-build vanilla JS + CSS served as static files by the server. No bundler, no framework, no client-side state store.
SPEC.md:29:All entities carry `id` (short random slug with type prefix: `sp_`, `si_`, `t_`, `day_` (day ids are `day_YYYY-MM-DD`), `idea_`), `created_at`, `updated_at` (unix seconds). All state changes append a row to an `events` table (`id` AUTOINCREMENT, `entity_id`, `kind`, `payload` JSON, `created_at`). Events are append-only: the code exposes no update or delete path for events.
SPEC.md:33:- Kickoff fields (markdown text): `limiting_factor`, `primary_bet`, `supports`, `premortem`. Frozen after `kickoff_frozen_at` is set — writes rejected except `weekly_addenda` (append-only list of `{date, text}`).

SPEC.md:40:- `blocked` status carries `blocked_by`: a non-empty list of ticket ids. When every blocker reaches ticket-state `done`, the item is flagged `blockers_cleared = true` (derived, not a status change).
SPEC.md:41:- Agent-permitted direct transitions: `todo ↔ active`, and setting `blocked` with blocker links. `done` and `deferred_next_sprint` require a proposal accepted by the human (Section 4.4 mechanics apply, with the item's single gating field being `status`).

SPEC.md:48:- `id = day_YYYY-MM-DD` where the date is the **planning date**: local time shifted by the boundary hour (Section 6.1). Day rows are created on first read or write for a date; reading a nonexistent day materializes it empty. There is no "missing day" state.

SPEC.md:63:`needs_success → needs_approach → needs_plan → in_progress → needs_review → done`, plus `dropped` (terminal, reachable from any non-`done` state, human-only action).
SPEC.md:65:There is no stored "ready" or "satisfied" state. Derived views (Section 4.5) express them.
SPEC.md:69:`tickets.fields` is a JSON object with exactly four keys: `success`, `approach`, `plan`, `result`. Each is `{value: string|null, proposal: {body, proposed_by, created_at}|null, notes: string|null}`. `value` is canonical and only ever written by the resolution engine (Section 4.4). `notes` is free-guidance text ("when doing the approach, consider X"), writable directly by human or agent at any time (event-logged, no state effect).
SPEC.md:71:Each pre-terminal state has one **gating field**:
SPEC.md:73:| state | gating field | accepted proposal advances to |
SPEC.md:81:The `result.notes` slot is the ticket's **review notes**: the specific things that need careful checking, written during planning and updated as review happens. Review itself is not a new mechanism: in `needs_review`, an agent orchestrates the Codex CLI against the result and these notes, fixes what review surfaces, and updates the notes — then the human approves. No extra states, entities, or flow machinery for this.
SPEC.md:86:- `ceiling` is the highest state an agent-caused advancement may move the ticket **into**. Below the ceiling, agents work freely: proposals on the current gating field auto-accept and advance (Section 4.4). Human actions ignore the ceiling entirely (the human may jump a ticket to any state, including straight to `done`; skipped gating fields simply keep `value = null`).
SPEC.md:92:The only write path for `fields.*.value` and for agent-caused state changes. Semantics, exactly:
SPEC.md:95:2. If the field is the current state's gating field AND the advance-target state ≤ `ceiling`: the proposal auto-accepts — `value` ← proposal body, proposal cleared, state advances one step, events `proposal_accepted` + `state_changed` logged with `resolved_by: "auto"`.
SPEC.md:99:6. Every transition writes exactly one `state_changed` event `{from, to, cause}`. One canonical function per transition edge in the code; no second writer.
SPEC.md:100:7. **The onward grant.** Human acceptance of a gating-field proposal (accept or edit-accept) requires the explicit grant pair: `next_ceiling` — any state at or beyond the newly entered state, or `none` meaning the ceiling becomes exactly the newly entered state — AND `at_cap` (`stop`: agents don't even propose from here; `propose`: agents may draft the next gating field for approval). Both are stated on every accept, even when the answer is "nothing further"; an accept without the pair is rejected with a structured error. Agent auto-accepts change neither ceiling nor at_cap. The `needs_review` approve action and sprint-item status accepts carry no onward grant (they are terminal for agent involvement).
